## Summary

Snapback 4 is one of two parallel candidates for the successor to Snapback 2,
and the sibling of Snapback 3, not its successor. It builds
[LLP 2000.000](./2000.000-keep-the-core-flip-two-substrates.rfc.md) as
written: the Snapback 2 core kept verbatim, two substrates flipped up front —
resources metered at runtime instead of priced statically, and a client that
replicates the viewer's declared partition instead of caching receipted
results — proved first on the hub fixture, with a deterministic simulator as
the primary harness. Snapback 3 ([LLP 2000](./2000-snapback3.rfc.md), led by
Codex / GPT-6 Astra) keeps the same two questions open and discovers their
answers in its first slice. Snapback 2 ([LLP 1000](./1000-snapback2.explainer.md))
continues as the incumbent and the control. All three are judged by one
pre-registered comparison on one clock.

This document records the author's decisions of 2026-09-12 that created the
track, restates the bet on one page so a reader can orient without the brief,
fixes the bakeoff protocol, draws the fence, and plans the first four weeks.
The design argument and its evidence stay in LLP 2000.000. Only running code
will bind (`rules/RULES.md`).

## 1. Decisions (author, 2026-09-12)

On 2026-09-12 Charlie Cheever asked whether Claude Fable 5.1 should lead a
Snapback 4 while Codex / GPT-6 Astra leads Snapback 3 and Snapback 2
continues, "and see which one turns out best." The lead answered yes with
conditions; the author accepted them ("Sounds good") and asked for this
document. The decisions below are therefore the lead's proposal as accepted
by the author. Where the author has not separately ruled, the text says so.

1. **Three tracks, one comparison.** Snapback 2 continues as the incumbent
   and the control. Snapback 3 builds LLP 2000; Snapback 4 builds LLP
   2000.000. This is a bakeoff of *bets* — discover the substrates in the
   slice versus decide them first — not of models. The result will say more
   about the bets than about the leads, and §3 is written so that it can.
2. **The name is Snapback 4.** Siblings, not a sequence: "4" does not mean
   "after 3." A separate number gives the track its own corpus root,
   directory, package identity and data directory with no rename when the
   bakeoff resolves, and files never move in LLP. If Snapback 4 loses, it is
   a number that never shipped. "Snapback 3b" was considered and rejected:
   it reads as a variant of 3, and this is a different bet.
3. **Snapback 4 builds LLP 2000.000 as written.** Its §14 open questions are
   the lead's to decide inside the track, recorded in §7 as they are
   decided. The author rules on the bar, the comparison and promotion (§3).
4. **One owner on the core.** The lead writes the engine, the store and the
   client contract. Codex lanes build the fixture generator, the reference
   applications, the device witnesses and the periphery. Blind reviews from
   two model families are reserved for the two disclosure-sensitive
   mechanisms: partition inversion of read rules (LLP 2000.000 §7.1) and
   the meter's cut behaviour on non-uniform scans (LLP 2000.000 §6). This
   departs from the standing rule that Codex codes and Claude orchestrates,
   deliberately, for the core only.
5. **The clock is four weeks** from the first commit under `snapback4/` to
   the first slice running on the hub fixture. At the clock the author kills
   the track, merges its substrates into a survivor, or continues it. There
   is no third month without a ruling.
6. **Shared and not shared.** Shared with Snapback 3: the LLP corpus, the
   Clique harness and scorers, the fixtures, the briefs, and the simulator
   once either track has one. Not shared: code, except the Snapback 2 pieces
   both are expected to copy (LLP 2000.000 §5.1).
7. **Snapback 2 keeps shipping and freezes new substrate work for the
   duration.** Fix-forward, the loop bar, acquisition and papercuts continue;
   no new certificate axes, client custody mechanisms or ceiling changes.
   This is accepted here as a condition of the bakeoff. Making it binding on
   concurrent Snapback 2 sessions needs one line in `rules/NOT-DOING.md`'s
   Moves ledger, which this document does not write.
8. **Reading load.** This root, updated in place, and one append-only
   journal, `snapback4/JOURNAL.md`. Sub-LLPs only for a decision that must
   outlive the journal. No panels beyond decision 4's two reviews.

## 2. The bet on one page

**Keep verbatim from Snapback 2** (LLP 2000.000 §5.1): the rule language
with its uniformity and hoist analysis, `decided_true` and the
structural-fact refusals; one data language, Quarry, with `--` comments,
`require … else CODE`, `shape`, `merge`, `walk`, `chunk`; the value model
and its conformance corpus; typed read and write states with no silent state
and the refusal envelope `{code, family, site, message, rewrite, guide}`; the
successor classifier (widening additive, checked narrowing, exact projection
ownership); `deliver`'s engine-computed audience, tombstones and
`bootstrap`; `schedule`, `job`, `chunk` and rehearsal; personas; `init`,
`dev`, `check`, `doctor`, `run`, `test`, `why`, `trace`, `guide`; H0
`deploy`, `rollback`, `secret`; channels; assets; the word-prefix index.

**Flip 1 — certify authorization, meter resources** (LLP 2000.000 §6). The
certificate keeps every read site's shape, uniformity, completeness class
and prediction eligibility, and stops pricing bytes and steps. A
deterministic meter charges examined rows, index entries, rule probes,
steps, heap, output and rows written as the program runs, on the server and
in the client interpreter alike. A query cut by the meter returns `capped`
with no continuation past the cut, the same observable outcome as a static
ceiling cut today; a mutation aborts with `E_BOUND` naming actual work; a
job is chunked. `check --cost` prints the structural cost class; `test`
prints measured meters on the hub fixture.

**Flip 2 — the viewer's partition is the unit of sync** (LLP 2000.000 §7).
`sync to <audience> last N by <index>` on a table makes the engine maintain,
per principal, the rows that principal may read, as a change log with a
horizon; a table without the word is online-only, and `check` says which
rule term prevented inversion. The client holds a replica of its partitions,
one watermark each, a durable outbox with client-minted ids, and the
interpreter; queries run locally and are `complete` inside the horizon.
Revocation is a scope tombstone; `until-revoked` and `delivered-history`
survive as the client's leave policy. Two client tiers — online-only
without a store, local-first with one — share one language and one server.

**The vocabulary is fixed before the mechanism** (LLP 2000.000 §8):

```ts
type Read<T> = { data: T; complete: boolean; fresh: boolean; since?: number; next?: Cursor }
            | { loading: true } | { denied: Refusal };
row.pending: boolean;
type Write = { state: "pending" } | { state: "sent"; seq: number } | { state: "failed"; why: Refusal };
type Link = "online" | "offline" | "signed-out";
```

A screen that needs a word not on that card is a defect in the mechanism,
not a gap in the card.

**The hub comes first** (LLP 2000.000 §11): the Clique generator's `u0001`
with 1,000 memberships, `u0500` with 500 follows, and about 100,000
messages are the first fixture; day-two changes are designed before the
day-one screen; the native client is in slice one; physical floors are
measured before targets; the simulator is the primary harness.

Shared with Snapback 3; LLP 2000 or a sibling record should adopt the same
text so that the two tracks are measured by one instrument.

(Clique) M1–M8, plus

The field-work application (LLP 2000 §4) tests generality after the first
slice, on whichever tracks survive the clock.

**Fixture.** The Clique generator at the instrument freeze LLP 1023 and
LLP 1024 record (`f40559f37722c79178be664a11f8a6485f1508fd`): hub `u0001`
with 100 groups and 900 DMs, `u0500` with 500 follows, and the 100,015

+//! Deployment purpose belongs to the store, never to application source or a seed plan.
+use std::path::Path;
+use crate::store_owner::Owner;
+
+pub(super) const KEY: &str = "deployment_environment";
+fn production() -> String {
+    "E_STAGING: existing production/legacy stores cannot be relabelled; deploy --staging into a separate fresh directory".into()
+}
+
+// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#1-commission-and-boundary — classify only a fresh store under its existing exclusive owner.
+pub(super) fn classify(dir: &Path, owner: Option<&Owner>, existed: bool, checking: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
+    let path = dir.join("store.sqlite");
+    if checking && !existed { return Ok(()); }
+    if let Some(owner) = owner {
+        let store = owner.open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
+        match store.meta(KEY).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() {
+            Some("staging") => Ok(()),
+            None if !existed && !checking => store.set_meta(KEY, "staging").map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
+            _ => Err(production()),
+        }
+    } else {
+        let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
+        if store.meta(KEY).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() == Some("staging") { Ok(()) } else { Err(production()) }
+    }
+}

+use super::*;
+
+const SOURCE: &str = "use identity\ntable items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\njob mark():\n  for item in items:\n    update items[item] { n: 9 }\n";
+
+fn close_doors(shared: &Shared, engine: &mut Engine) {
+    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), SOURCE.replace("use identity", "use identity password"))]);
+    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
+    let next = engine.adopt(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone())).unwrap();
+    lock(&shared.readers).replace(engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
+    *lock(&shared.bound) = bound;
+    *lock(&shared.generation) = next;
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn stage_slow_dev_bodies_cannot_retain_authority_after_password_adoption() {
+    for (path, payload) in [("/seed", "[]"), ("/dev/reset", "{}"), ("/dev/clock/advance", r#"{"duration":"+1h"}"#), ("/dev/jobs/mark", "{}")] {
+        let host = Host::with_default_doors(SOURCE);
+        let before = host.shared.now();
+        let mut stream = TcpStream::connect(host.address).unwrap();
+        stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5))).unwrap();
+        write!(stream, "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nExpect: 100-continue\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n", payload.len()).unwrap();
+        let mut interim = [0; 128];
+        let n = stream.read(&mut interim).unwrap();
+        assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&interim[..n]).contains("100 Continue"), "passed the pre-body door fence");
+        close_doors(&host.shared, &mut lock(&host.shared.engine));
+        stream.write_all(payload.as_bytes()).unwrap();
+        let mut response = String::new(); stream.read_to_string(&mut response).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH", "{path}: {response}");
+        assert!(host.shared.now() - before < 5000, "refused clock did not advance");
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn stage_queued_dev_requests_recheck_authority_at_the_writer() {
+    for (path, payload) in [("/seed", "[]"), ("/dev/reset", "{}"), ("/dev/clock/advance", r#"{"duration":"+1h"}"#), ("/dev/jobs/mark", "{}")] {
+        let host = Host::with_default_doors(SOURCE);
+        let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
+        let address = host.address;
+        let task = thread::spawn(move || exchange_held(address, Duration::from_secs(10), &format!("POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{payload}", payload.len())));
+        wait_until(|| host.shared.engine.waiting() > 0);
+        close_doors(&host.shared, &mut engine);
+        drop(engine);
+        let response = task.join().unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH", "{path}: {response}");
+    }
+}

+//! Deployment purpose belongs to the store, never to application source or a seed plan.
+use std::path::Path;
+use crate::store_owner::Owner;
+
+pub(super) const KEY: &str = "deployment_environment";
+fn production() -> String {
+    "E_STAGING: existing production/legacy stores cannot be relabelled; deploy --staging into a separate fresh directory".into()
+}
+
+// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#1-commission-and-boundary — classify only a fresh store under its existing exclusive owner.
+pub(super) fn classify(dir: &Path, owner: Option<&Owner>, existed: bool, checking: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
+    let path = dir.join("store.sqlite");
+    if checking && !existed { return Ok(()); }
+    if let Some(owner) = owner {
+        let store = owner.open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
+        match store.meta(KEY).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() {
+            Some("staging") => Ok(()),
+            None if !existed && !checking => store.set_meta(KEY, "staging").map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
+            _ => Err(production()),
+        }
+    } else {
+        let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
+        if store.meta(KEY).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() == Some("staging") { Ok(()) } else { Err(production()) }
+    }
+}

import { randomUUID, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import type { ServeHost } from "./proof.ts";

export interface StagingAccount {
  role: string; email: string; password: string; principal: string; token: string; expiresAt: number;
}
export type StagingSessions = Record<string, StagingAccount[]>;
export interface SeedAccountsOptions {
  roles: Record<string, number>;
  provision: { readonly name: string };
  args?: (account: StagingAccount) => Record<string, unknown>;
  /** Whole setup deadline, including rate-limit waits. Default 5 minutes. */
  timeoutMs?: number;
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — real credentials and ordinary operator mutations, never system seeding or auth bypass.
export async function seedAccounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken">, options: SeedAccountsOptions): Promise<StagingSessions> {
  const roles = Object.entries(options.roles), timeout = options.timeoutMs ?? 300_000;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(timeout) || timeout < 1 || !roles.length || roles.some(([role, n]) => !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}$/.test(role) || !Number.isSafeInteger(n) || n < 1)
      || roles.reduce((sum, [, n]) => sum + n, 0) > 100 || !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*(?:\.[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*)*$/.test(options.provision?.name ?? "")) {
    throw Error("E_STAGING_INPUT: provide 1..100 accounts across named roles, a mutation handle and a positive timeoutMs");
  }
  const end = performance.now() + timeout;
  const remaining = () => {
    const ms = Math.ceil(end - performance.now());
    if (ms <= 0) throw Error("E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: setup incomplete; partial accounts/grants may exist; inspect or discard this staging deployment");
    return ms;
  };
  const request = (path: string, init?: RequestInit) => fetch(host.url + path, { ...init, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(Math.min(remaining(), 30_000)) });
  const config = await request("/auth/config");
  const identity = await config.json();
  if (!config.ok || identity.environment !== "staging" || identity.development !== false || !identity.password) {
    throw Error("E_STAGING: account seeding requires serve on a fresh deploy --staging with password identity; production and dev refuse before creating users");
  }
  const operator = await host.operatorToken();
  const sessions: StagingSessions = Object.create(null);
  for (const [role, count] of roles) {
    sessions[role] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const email = `staging-${role}-${randomUUID()}@example.test`, password = randomBytes(24).toString("base64url");
      let reply;
      // Only an explicit HTTP 429 refusal authorizes retrying signup. Network
      // failures and malformed/ambiguous successful replies are never retried.
      for (;;) {
        const response = await request("/auth/signup", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }) });
        reply = await response.json();
        if (response.status !== 429 || reply.denied?.code !== "E_RATE_LIMIT") break;
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(1000, remaining())));
      }
      const session = reply.session;
      if (typeof session?.principal !== "string" || typeof session.token !== "string" || typeof session.expiresAt !== "number") {
        throw Error(`E_STAGING_SIGNUP: signup refused (${reply.denied?.code ?? "invalid reply"}); partial accounts/grants may exist`);
      }
      const account: StagingAccount = { role, email, password, principal: session.principal, token: session.token, expiresAt: session.expiresAt };
      const args = options.args ? options.args(account) : { principal: account.principal, role };
      const response = await request(`/m/${options.provision.name}`, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", authorization: `Bearer ${operator}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ id: randomUUID(), args }) });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok || result.state !== "sent") throw Error(`E_STAGING_PROVISION: ${options.provision.name} refused (${result.why?.code ?? result.denied?.code ?? "invalid reply"}); partial accounts/grants may exist`);
      sessions[role]!.push(account);
    }
  }
  return sessions;
}

test("staging accounts are real password users; service grants obey rules; marker survives release switches", { timeout: 90000 }, async t => {
  const cwd = fixture(t);
  await deploy("staging", "snapback", { cwd, args: ["--staging"] });
  let host = await startServe("staging", { cwd, seedUsers });
  try {
    assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "staging");
    assert.equal((await config(host)).development, false);
    for (const [role, accounts] of Object.entries(host.sessions)) {
      assert.equal(accounts.length, 2);
      for (const account of accounts) {
        assert.ok(account.principal.startsWith("user:"));
        const signed = await login(host.url, account.email, account.password); assert.equal(signed.ok, true);
        const me = await post(host, "/q/myRole", signed.session.token); assert.equal(me.data.role, role);
        assert.equal((await post(host, "/m/add", signed.session.token, { body: "real user data" })).state, "sent");
        const forged = await post(host, "/m/provision", signed.session.token, { principal: account.principal, role: "admin" });
        assert.equal(forged.why.code, "NOT_OPERATOR");
      }
    }
    for (const path of ["/seed", "/dev/reset", "/dev/clock/advance", "/dev/jobs/foo", "/dev/token"]) {
      assert.equal((await fetch(host.url + path, { method: "POST", body: "[]" })).status, 403, path);
    }
    assert.equal((await fetch(host.url + "/q/myRole", { method: "POST", headers: { "x-snapback-persona": "alice" }, body: '{"args":{}}' })).status, 401);
    const v2 = join(cwd, "v2"); mkdirSync(v2); writeFileSync(join(v2, "schema.q"), schema + "\nquery version():\n  return 2\n");
    await deploy("staging", v2, { cwd }); assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "staging");
    await rollback("staging", 1, { cwd }); assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "staging");
    const repeated = await cli(["deploy", "staging", "--schema", "snapback", "--staging"], { cwd });
    assert.equal(repeated.code, 0, repeated.stderr);
    const token = await host.operatorToken();
    for (const file of readdirSync(join(cwd, "logs"))) assert.ok(!readFileSync(join(cwd, "logs", file), "utf8").includes(token), "operator token never enters logs");
  } finally { await host.stop(); }
  host = await startServe("staging", { cwd });
  try { assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "staging"); } finally { await host.stop(); }
});

test("production cannot be relabelled; bulk setup refuses before token mint or account creation; first admin uses explicit operator", { timeout: 60000 }, async t => {
  const cwd = fixture(t);
  await deploy("production", "snapback", { cwd });
  let host = await startServe("production", { cwd });
  try {
    assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "production");
    let minted = false;
    await assert.rejects(seedAccounts({ url: host.url, operatorToken: async () => { minted = true; return "must not mint"; } }, seedUsers), /E_STAGING:/);
    assert.equal(minted, false);
    const relabel = await cli(["deploy", "production", "--staging"], { cwd });
    assert.notEqual(relabel.code, 0); assert.match(relabel.stderr, /E_STAGING.*cannot be relabelled/);
    const account = await signup(host.url, "first@example.test", "operator-selected-password"); assert.equal(account.ok, true);
    assert.equal((await post(host, "/q/myRole", account.session.token)).data, null, "signup grants no role");
    assert.equal((await post(host, "/m/provision", account.session.token, { principal: account.session.principal, role: "admin" })).why.code, "NOT_OPERATOR");
    assert.equal((await post(host, "/m/provision", await host.operatorToken(), { principal: account.session.principal, role: "admin" })).state, "sent");
    assert.equal((await post(host, "/q/myRole", account.session.token)).data.role, "admin");
  } finally { await host.stop(); }
  await assert.rejects(startServe("production", { cwd, seedUsers }), /E_STAGING:/, "failed setup cleans up its own host");
  const relabel = await cli(["deploy", "production", "--staging"], { cwd }); assert.notEqual(relabel.code, 0);
});

test("password dev closes fixture and persona doors; explicit opt-in, declared cast, and persistent defaults", { timeout: 90000 }, async t => {
  const cwd = fixture(t);
  let host = await startDev({ cwd, args: ["--no-watch"] });
  try {
    assert.deepEqual(await config(host).then(({ development, devDoors, environment }) => ({ development, devDoors, environment })), { development: true, devDoors: false, environment: "development" });
    for (const path of ["/seed", "/dev/status", "/dev/catalog", "/dev/reset", "/dev/clock/advance", "/dev/jobs/foo"]) {
      const response = await fetch(host.url + path, { method: path.endsWith("status") || path.endsWith("catalog") ? "GET" : "POST", body: path.endsWith("status") || path.endsWith("catalog") ? undefined : "[]" });
      assert.equal(response.status, 403, path);
    }
    await assert.rejects(host.now(), /unsafe-dev-doors/);
    const account = await signup(host.url, "persistent@example.test", "persistent-password"); assert.equal(account.ok, true);
  } finally { await host.stop(); }
  host = await startDev({ cwd, args: ["--unsafe-dev-doors", "--no-watch"] });
  try {
    assert.equal((await config(host)).devDoors, true);
    assert.equal((await login(host.url, "persistent@example.test", "persistent-password")).ok, true, "default dev preserves password verifiers across restart");
    assert.equal((await fetch(host.url + "/dev/status")).status, 200);
    assert.equal((await fetch(host.url + "/q/myRole", { method: "POST", headers: { "x-snapback-persona": "undeclared" }, body: '{"args":{}}' })).status, 401, "empty cast admits no persona");
  } finally { await host.stop(); }
  host = await startDev({ cwd, args: ["--memory", "--no-watch"] });
  try { assert.equal((await login(host.url, "persistent@example.test", "persistent-password")).ok, false, "memory explicitly isolates accounts"); }
  finally { await host.stop(); }
});

test("adopting password-only identity closes existing dev doors and declared personas", { timeout: 60000 }, async t => {

    writeFileSync(join(cwd, "snapback/schema.q"), schema + "\nuse personas alice\n");
    const end = Date.now() + 10000;

    assert.equal((await config(host)).devDoors, false);
    assert.equal((await fetch(host.url + "/seed", { method: "POST", body: "[]" })).status, 403);
    assert.equal((await fetch(host.url + "/q/myRole", { method: "POST", headers: { "x-snapback-persona": "alice" }, body: '{"args":{}}' })).status, 401);
  } finally { await host.stop(); }
});
use super::*;

const SOURCE: &str = "use identity\ntable items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\njob mark():\n  for item in items:\n    update items[item] { n: 9 }\n";

fn close_doors(shared: &Shared, engine: &mut Engine) {
    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), SOURCE.replace("use identity", "use identity password"))]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
    let next = engine.adopt(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone())).unwrap();
    lock(&shared.readers).replace(engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
    *lock(&shared.bound) = bound;
    *lock(&shared.generation) = next;
}

#[test]
fn stage_slow_dev_bodies_cannot_retain_authority_after_password_adoption() {
    for (path, payload) in [("/seed", "[]"), ("/dev/reset", "{}"), ("/dev/clock/advance", r#"{"duration":"+1h"}"#), ("/dev/jobs/mark", "{}")] {
        let host = Host::with_default_doors(SOURCE);
        let before = host.shared.now();
        let mut stream = TcpStream::connect(host.address).unwrap();
        stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5))).unwrap();
        write!(stream, "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nExpect: 100-continue\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n", payload.len()).unwrap();
        let mut interim = [0; 128];
        let n = stream.read(&mut interim).unwrap();
        assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&interim[..n]).contains("100 Continue"), "passed the pre-body door fence");
        close_doors(&host.shared, &mut lock(&host.shared.engine));
        stream.write_all(payload.as_bytes()).unwrap();
        let mut response = String::new(); stream.read_to_string(&mut response).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH", "{path}: {response}");
        assert!(host.shared.now() - before < 5000, "refused clock did not advance");
    }
}

#[test]
fn stage_queued_dev_requests_recheck_authority_at_the_writer() {
    for (path, payload) in [("/seed", "[]"), ("/dev/reset", "{}"), ("/dev/clock/advance", r#"{"duration":"+1h"}"#), ("/dev/jobs/mark", "{}")] {
        let host = Host::with_default_doors(SOURCE);
        let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
        let address = host.address;
        let task = thread::spawn(move || exchange_held(address, Duration::from_secs(10), &format!("POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{payload}", payload.len())));
        wait_until(|| host.shared.engine.waiting() > 0);
        close_doors(&host.shared, &mut engine);
        drop(engine);
        let response = task.join().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH", "{path}: {response}");
    }
}

//! Immutable source releases with a database-authoritative activation pointer.
use super::*;
use crate::store_owner::Owner;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
#[path = "staging.rs"]
mod staging;
struct Stage(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Stage {fn drop(&mut self){let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}

fn directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    options
        .positionals
        .first()
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .ok_or_else(|| "a deployment directory is required".into())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5.1 — deployment aliases share ownership, control and worker paths.
fn canonical_directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    let requested = directory(options)?;
    if !requested.exists() {
        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&requested).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    let dir = requested.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    Ok(dir)
}

/// `dev` and `serve` hold the store-owner lock for their lifetime. Release
/// commands route to the admitted server when it owns this deployment.
pub(crate) fn deployment_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> {
    crate::store_owner::command(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))
}
fn current(dir: &Path, owner: &Owner) -> Result<Option<u64>, String> {
    let database = dir.join("store.sqlite");
    if database.exists() {
        let store = owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if let Some(release) = store.meta("release").map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
            return release
                .parse()
                .map(Some)
                .map_err(|_| "invalid release identity in store".into());
        }
    }
    // Compatibility with deployments made before the database owned this
    // pointer. The next activation records it transactionally.
    match std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("current")) {
        Ok(text) => text
            .trim()
            .parse()
            .map(Some)
            .map_err(|_| "invalid current release file".into()),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
        Err(e) => Err(format!("cannot read current release: {e}")),
    }
}
pub(super) fn bound_at(root: PathBuf) -> Result<snapback4_lang::Bound, String> {
    let project = Project { root };
    if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
        return Err("release has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release".into());
    }
    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return Err(bound
            .diagnostics
            .iter()
            .map(ToString::to_string)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("\n"));
    }
    Ok(bound)
}
fn check_change(
    dir: &Path,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound,
    options: &Options,
    target_release: Option<u64>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    if !options.flags.contains_key("check") && let Some(n) = previous {
        let old = bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))?;
        let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let target_label = target_release.map(|n| format!("release {n}")).unwrap_or_else(|| "the new release".into());
        let lines = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &old.schema, &bound.schema, n, &target_label, target_release.is_none())?;
        for line in lines {
            eprintln!("{line}");
        }
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("allow-destructive") {
        eprintln!(
            "deploy/rollback retain storage; --allow-destructive does not bypass validation or prune. To drop dormant storage: snapback4 prune {} --allow-destructive",
            dir.display()
        );
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn command_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<std::fs::File, String> {
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(
            &dir.join("store.sqlite"),
            &dir.join(".release-command"),
            true,
            0,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        file.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        Ok(file)
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    Err("release commands require Unix".into())
}
fn target(dir: &Path) -> Result<Option<Owner>, String> {
    match deployment_lock(dir) {
        Ok(owner) => Ok(Some(owner)),
        Err(error) if error.contains("E_STORE_BUSY") => Ok(None),
        Err(error) => Err(error),
    }
}
fn status(
    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,
    after: u64,
    items_after: Option<String>,
    event: Option<u64>,
) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    match owner {
        Some(owner) => owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .and_then(|mut s| s.release_status(after, items_after.as_deref(), event))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        None => crate::release_control::request(
            dir,
            crate::release_control::Command::Status {
                after,
                items_after,
                event,
            },
        ),
    }
}
fn activate(
    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,
    release: u64,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    revision: u64,
    bound: snapback4_lang::Bound,
    rollback: bool,
    restore_dormant: bool,
    allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    options: &Options,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let root = dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string());
    let digest = crate::release_files::digest(&root)?;
    if let Some(owner) = owner {
        let project = Project { root };
        let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
        crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
        crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
        let mut pointer = crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, previous, rollback)?;
        let activation = snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation {
            release,
            expected_previous: previous,
            expected_revision: Some(revision),
            restore_dormant,
            native_digest: prepared.installed.as_ref().map(|m| m.digest.clone()),
            operation: if rollback { "rollback" } else { "deploy" }.into(),
        };
        let store = owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let engine = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared(
            store,
            bound.schema,
            bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
            &activation,
            allow_feed_adoption,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        pointer.finish(engine.generation())?;
        receipt(release,engine.generation(),&engine.store().adoption_report,options);

    } else {
        let result = crate::release_control::request(
            dir,
            crate::release_control::Command::Activate {
                release,
                expected_release: previous,
                expected_revision: revision,
                digest,
                rollback,
                restore_dormant,
                allow_feed_adoption,
            },
        )?;
        if options.json {println!("{}",result);} else {
            println!("release {release} is current as generation {}; {}; running serve adopted the release",result["generation"],result["adoption"].as_str().unwrap_or("adopted"));
            for fill in result["backfills"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().filter_map(|f|f.as_str()) {println!("  backfill: {fill}");}
            if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {println!("{}",result["details"].as_str().unwrap_or(""));}
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn report(verb: &str, result: Result<(), String>) -> i32 {
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — release completion owns no engine/lease; bound the final diagnostic drain.
    // The background writer must get a chance to emit adoption estimates before
    // this short-lived command exits. Broken/stalled output cannot hold exit.
    let _ = snapback4_core::diagnostics::drain(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3));
    match result {
        Ok(()) => 0,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("{verb}: {error}");
            1
        }
    }
}

pub fn deploy(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "deploy",
        (|| {
            let dir = canonical_directory(options)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let existed = dir.join("store.sqlite").exists();
            let checking=options.flags.contains_key("check");
            let _deployment = if checking {None} else {target(&dir)?};
            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
            let project = super::proof_source::project(options, "schema")?;
            let sources = super::proof_source::sources(&project, options, "schema")?;
            let bound = compiled_or_exit(super::proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "schema"), options.json);
            if options.flags.contains_key("staging") {
                staging::classify(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), existed, checking)?;
            }
            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("releases")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let held = if checking {
                if dir.join("store.sqlite").exists() {snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).and_then(|mut s|s.release_status(0,None,None)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?}
                else {serde_json::json!({"release":null,"revision":0,"generation":1})}
            } else {status(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?};
            let previous = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
                current(&dir, owner)?
            } else {
                held["release"].as_u64()
            };
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            check_change(&dir, previous, &bound, options, None)?;
            let releases = dir.join("releases").canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let stage = releases.join(format!(".stage-{}", std::process::id()));
            if stage.exists() {std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
            std::fs::create_dir(&stage).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let _cleanup=Stage(stage.clone());
            std::fs::create_dir(stage.join("snapback")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let source_hash = {
                use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
                let mut hash = Sha256::new();
                let mut ordered = sources.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
                ordered.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
                for (name,text) in ordered {
                    hash.update((name.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(name.as_bytes());
                    hash.update((text.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(text.as_bytes());
                }
                format!("{:x}", hash.finalize())
            };
            for (name,text) in sources {
                let path=stage.join("snapback").join(name);
                if let Some(parent)=path.parent(){std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
                std::fs::write(path,text).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            }
            crate::dev_worker::package(&project, &stage)?;
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — saved schemas prepare companion types in a private snapshot, never in working generated/.
            let private = if options.flags.contains_key("schema") || crate::dev_worker::source(&project).is_some() {
                Some(super::proof_source::Candidate::prepare(&project, &stage, &bound)?)
            } else { None };
            let build_project = private.as_ref().map(|candidate| &candidate.project).unwrap_or(&project);
            // A native job's module travels with the captured source.
            if let Some(built) =
                crate::native::build(build_project, &bound).map_err(|p| p.to_string())?
            {
                let generated = stage.join("snapback").join("generated");
                std::fs::create_dir_all(&generated).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                std::fs::copy(&built.path, generated.join("native.wasm"))
                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
            std::fs::write(stage.join("device-compatibility.json"), serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&bound.schema.public_projection())).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(build_project, &stage)?;
            if let Some(previous)=previous {
                if held["release"].as_u64()==Some(previous) && crate::release_files::digest(&stage)?==crate::release_files::digest(&releases.join(previous.to_string()))? {
                    std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
                    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"unchanged":true,"release":previous,"generation":held["generation"]}));}
                    else {println!("unchanged; release {previous} stays current as generation {}",held["generation"]);}
                    return Ok(());
                }
            }
            let mut n=std::fs::read_dir(&releases).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.filter_map(Result::ok).filter_map(|e|e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok()).max().unwrap_or(0);
            let staged_bound = bound_at(stage.clone())?;
            if options.flags.contains_key("check") {
                let mut preview_options=options.clone();preview_options.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
                let result=super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),stage.clone(),n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?,&preview_options);
                let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage);
                return result;
            }
            let final_dir=loop {
                n=n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?;
                let path=releases.join(n.to_string());
                match std::fs::create_dir(&path) {Ok(())=>break path,Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists=>continue,Err(e)=>return Err(e.to_string())}
            };
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#10-wave-10-release-lifecycle-amendment — only committed adoption makes a candidate selectable.
            std::fs::write(stage.join(".pending-adoption"), b"pending\n").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            if staged_bound.schema != bound.schema {
                return Err("staged source does not match the compiled schema".into());
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#4-cli-surface-contracts — provenance is immutable metadata, excluded from executable identity so identical deploys stay no-ops.
            std::fs::write(stage.join("provenance.json"), format!("{}\n", serde_json::json!({
                "release": n, "sourceHash": source_hash, "cliVersion": env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"), "createdAt": snapback4_core::now_ms()
            }))).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            crate::release_files::durable(&stage)?;
            std::fs::rename(&stage, &final_dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            std::fs::File::open(&releases)
                .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let result=activate(
                &dir,
                _deployment.as_ref(),
                n,
                previous,
                revision,
                staged_bound,
                false,
                options.flags.contains_key("restore-dormant"),
                options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
                options,
            );
            match result {
                Ok(()) => { if let Err(error)=std::fs::remove_file(final_dir.join(".pending-adoption")) {eprintln!("release {n} committed; pending-marker cleanup failed: {error}; history proves adoption");} }
                Err(error) => {
                    // Keep the source reserved even after a lost reply; committed history is authoritative.
                    let matching_latest=latest(&dir).ok().filter(|id|crate::release_files::digest(&final_dir).ok()==crate::release_files::digest(&releases.join(id.to_string())).ok());
                    let error=if error.contains("dormant names") && matching_latest.is_some() {
                        format!("E_ADOPT: source matches latest; use `snapback4 rollback {} --to latest` to reactivate it; alternatively `snapback4 deploy {} --restore-dormant`. {error}",dir.display(),dir.display())
                    } else {error};
                    if options.json && error.contains("dormant names") {
                        let mut inventory=Vec::new();let mut cursor=None;
                        loop {
                            let page=status(&dir,_deployment.as_ref(),0,cursor,None)?;
                            let items=page["items"].as_array().ok_or("invalid dormant inventory response")?;
                            if items.is_empty() {break;}
                            inventory.extend(items.iter().cloned());
                            cursor=items.last().and_then(|i|i["key"].as_str()).map(str::to_owned);
                            if cursor.is_none() {break;}
                        }
                        println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"ok":false,"code":"E_ADOPT","message":error,"dormant":inventory}));
                    }
                    return Err(error);
                }
            }
            Ok(())
        })(),
    )
}

pub fn rollback(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "rollback",
        (|| {
            let dir = directory(options)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let _deployment = target(&dir)?;
            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
            let held = status(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?;
            let active = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
                current(&dir, owner)?
            } else {
                held["release"].as_u64()
            }
            .ok_or("no current release")?;
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            let selected = options
                .flags
                .get("to")
                .map(|n| {
                    if n=="latest" {return latest(&dir).map_err(|_| "no complete latest release");}
                    n.parse::<u64>()
                        .ok()
                        .filter(|n| *n > 0)
                        .ok_or("--to requires a positive immutable release id")
                })
                .transpose()?;
            let previous = selected
                .or_else(|| held["previous_active"].as_u64())
                .ok_or("nothing to roll back to")?;
            selectable(&dir, previous)?;
            let bound = bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(previous.to_string()))?;
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#14-wave-14-rollback-narration — resolve bare/latest selection visibly before any preview or live activation.
            let target_line=format!("resolved rollback target: release {previous} (current release {active})");
            if options.json {eprintln!("{target_line}");} else {println!("{target_line}");}
            check_change(&dir, Some(active), &bound, options, Some(previous))?;
            if options.flags.contains_key("check") {
                return super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),dir.join("releases").join(previous.to_string()),previous,options);
            }
            activate(
                &dir,
                _deployment.as_ref(),
                previous,
                Some(active),
                revision,
                bound,
                true,
                true,
                options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
                options,
            )?;

            Ok(())
        })(),
    )
}

pub fn releases(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "releases",
        (|| {
            let dir = directory(options)?;
            let after = options
                .flags
                .get("after")
                .map(|s| s.parse::<u64>().map_err(|_| "--after requires an event id"))
                .transpose()?
                .unwrap_or(0);
            let event = options
                .flags
                .get("event")
                .map(|s| s.parse::<u64>().map_err(|_| "--event requires an event id"))
                .transpose()?;
            // Read-only inspection needs no ownership handoff or source adoption.
            let mut store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let mut result = store
                .release_status(
                    after,
                    options.flags.get("items-after").map(String::as_str),
                    event,
                )
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            result["latest"]=serde_json::json!(latest(&dir).ok());
            if let Some(selector)=options.positionals.get(1) {
                let n=if selector=="latest" {latest(&dir)?} else {selector.parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or("releases selection requires N or latest")?};
                selectable(&dir,n)?;let root=dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string());bound_at(root.clone())?;
                result["selected"]=serde_json::json!({"release":n,"current":result["release"].as_u64()==Some(n),"digest":crate::release_files::digest(&root)?});
            }
            println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&result).unwrap());
            Ok(())
        })(),
    )
}

pub fn prune(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "prune",
        (|| {
            let dir = directory(options)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let owner = target(&dir)?;
            let held = status(&dir, owner.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?;
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            let confirm = options.flags.contains_key("allow-destructive");
            let result = match owner.as_ref() {
                Some(owner) => owner
                    .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
                    .and_then(|mut s| s.prune_dormant(confirm, Some(revision)))
                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
                None => crate::release_control::request(
                    &dir,
                    crate::release_control::Command::Prune {
                        confirm,
                        expected_revision: revision,
                    },
                )?,
            };
            println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&result).unwrap());
            // Live responses are bounded. Enumerate the committed audit set (or
            // current preview) to name every item, never silently omit a drop.
            if owner.is_none() {
                let mut after = result["items_after"].as_str().map(str::to_owned);
                while let Some(key) = after {
                    let page = status(&dir, None, 0, Some(key), result["event"].as_u64())?;
                    let items = page["items"].as_array().ok_or("invalid inventory reply")?;
                    if items.is_empty() {
                        break;
                    }
                    println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(items).unwrap());
                    after = page["items_after"].as_str().map(str::to_owned);
                }
            }
            if !confirm {
                println!(
                    "preview only; to permanently drop this dormant storage: snapback4 prune {} --allow-destructive",
                    dir.display()
                );
            }
            Ok(())
        })(),
    )
}

pub fn serve_release(options: &Options) -> Result<(), String> {
    let requested_port = crate::port_flag::parse("serve", options.flags.get("port").map(String::as_str))?;
    let bind = release_bind(options)?;
    let capacity = crate::host_limits::Capacity::from_options(&options.flags, |name| {
        std::env::var(name).ok()
    })?;
    let dir = canonical_directory(options)?;
    crate::host_limits::initialize();
    let owner = crate::store_owner::host(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))?;
    owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
    let current = current(&dir, &owner)?.ok_or("no current release; deploy first")?;
    let release = Project {
        root: dir.join("releases").join(current.to_string())
            .canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    };
    crate::release_files::link_store(&release.root)?;
    let (port, listener) = crate::port_flag::listen("serve", requested_port, bind, None)?;
    let worker = options
        .flags
        .get("worker")
        .map(|path| {
            Path::new(path)
                .canonicalize()
                .map_err(|e| format!("--worker {path}: {e}"))
        })
        .transpose()?;
    crate::serve::serve(
        release,
        port,
        crate::serve::Mode::Release {
            expected: current,
            worker,
            owner: std::sync::Arc::new(owner),
        },
        &bind.to_string(),
        listener,
        capacity,
    )
}

pub(crate) fn release_bind(options: &Options) -> Result<std::net::IpAddr, String> {
    let bind = options
        .flags
        .get("bind")
        .map(String::as_str)
        .unwrap_or("127.0.0.1");
    let address: std::net::IpAddr = bind
        .parse()
        .map_err(|_| "--bind must be a literal IPv4 or IPv6 address".to_owned())?;
    if !address.is_loopback() {
        if !options.flags.contains_key("i-understand-cleartext") {
            return Err("non-loopback HTTP sends passwords and bearer tokens in cleartext; use a loopback listener behind a TLS proxy, or explicitly pass --i-understand-cleartext".into());
        }
        eprintln!(
            "WARNING: listening on {address} over CLEARTEXT HTTP. Passwords, bearer tokens, schema and data have no transport protection. Expose only through a TLS proxy on a trusted private hop; see snapback4/README.md."
        );
    }
    Ok(address)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn deployment_startup_resolves_new_paths_and_existing_aliases() {
        use std::os::unix::fs::{symlink, PermissionsExt};
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-canonical-release-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(), std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap().as_nanos()));
        std::fs::create_dir(&root).unwrap();
        struct Cleanup(std::path::PathBuf);
        impl Drop for Cleanup { fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0); } }
        let _cleanup = Cleanup(root.clone());
        let options = |path: &std::path::Path| super::parse(&["deploy".into(), path.to_str().unwrap().into()]);
        let target = root.join("deployment");
        let canonical = super::canonical_directory(&options(&target)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(canonical, target.canonicalize().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(std::fs::metadata(&canonical).unwrap().permissions().mode() & 0o777, 0o700);
        let alias = root.join("alias");
        symlink(&target, &alias).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(super::canonical_directory(&options(&alias)).unwrap(), canonical);
        let nested = alias.join("nested");
        assert_eq!(super::canonical_directory(&options(&nested)).unwrap(), canonical.join("nested"));
        let file = root.join("file"); std::fs::write(&file, "preserved").unwrap();
        assert!(super::canonical_directory(&options(&file)).is_err());
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(file).unwrap(), "preserved");
    }

    #[test]
    fn release_diagnostic_fixture() {
        let Ok(outcome) = std::env::var("SB4_RELEASE_DIAGNOSTIC_FIXTURE") else {
            return;
        };
        snapback4_core::diagnostics::set_thread_start(|| {
            std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(200))
        });
        snapback4_core::diagnostic!("release completion estimate");
        let failed = outcome == "failure";
        assert_eq!(
            super::report(
                "deploy",
                if failed {
                    Err("release fixture failure".into())
                } else {
                    Ok(())
                }
            ),
            i32::from(failed)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn release_completion_drains_a_delayed_writer_on_success_and_failure() {
        for outcome in ["success", "failure"] {
            let output = std::process::Command::new(std::env::current_exe().unwrap())
                .args([
                    "--exact",
                    "verbs::releases::tests::release_diagnostic_fixture",
                    "--nocapture",
                ])
                .env("SB4_RELEASE_DIAGNOSTIC_FIXTURE", outcome)
                .output()
                .unwrap();
            assert!(output.status.success(), "{output:?}");
            let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
            assert!(stderr.contains("release completion estimate"), "{stderr}");
            if outcome == "failure" {
                assert!(stderr.contains("release fixture failure"), "{stderr}");
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn serving_defaults_loopback_and_requires_cleartext_acknowledgement() {
        let parse =
            |args: &[&str]| super::parse(&args.iter().map(|s| (*s).to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>());
        assert!(
            super::release_bind(&parse(&["serve", "release"]))
                .unwrap()
                .is_loopback()
        );
        assert!(super::release_bind(&parse(&["serve", "release", "--bind", "0.0.0.0"])).is_err());
        assert!(super::release_bind(&parse(&["serve", "release", "--bind", "::"])).is_err());
        assert!(super::release_bind(&parse(&["serve", "release", "--bind", "localhost"])).is_err());
        let options = parse(&[
            "serve",
            "--i-understand-cleartext",
            "release",
            "--bind",
            "0.0.0.0",
        ]);
        assert_eq!(options.positionals, ["release"]);
        assert!(super::release_bind(&options).unwrap().is_unspecified());
    }
}

fn receipt(release:u64,generation:u64,report:&snapback4_core::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport,options:&Options){
    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"release":release,"generation":generation,"adoption":report.compact(),"details":report.summary(),"backfills":report.backfills}));}
    else {println!("release {release} is current as generation {generation}; {}",report.compact());for fill in &report.backfills {println!("  backfill: {fill}");}if options.flags.contains_key("verbose"){println!("{}",report.summary());}}
}
fn latest(dir:&Path)->Result<u64,String>{
    std::fs::read_dir(dir.join("releases")).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.filter_map(Result::ok)
        .filter_map(|e|e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|selectable(dir,*n).is_ok()))
        .max().ok_or("no complete immutable releases".into())
}
pub(super) fn check_release(options:&Options)->i32 {
    report("check",(|| {
        let store=PathBuf::from(options.flags.get("store").ok_or("--store required")?);
        let dir=store.parent().ok_or("store has no deployment directory")?;
        let selected=options.flags.get("release").ok_or("--release required")?;
        let n=if selected=="latest"{latest(dir)?}else{selected.parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or("--release requires N or latest")?};
        selectable(dir,n)?;
        super::release_preview::preview(&store,dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()),n,options)
    })())
}

fn selectable(dir:&Path,release:u64)->Result<(),String> {
    let root=dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string());
    let store=snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let history:bool=store.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE type='table' AND name='_release_events')",[],|r|r.get(0)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    if history || root.join(".pending-adoption").exists() {
        let committed=if history {store.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _release_events WHERE (release=?1 OR previous=?1) AND operation!='prune')",[i64::try_from(release).map_err(|_|"release identity exceeds SQLite range")?],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?} else {false};
        if !committed {return Err(format!("release {release} did not complete adoption; select a successful release with --to latest"));}
    }
    bound_at(root).map(|_|())
}
//! Ownership locks the database's sidecar; stat pins its inode. `serve.lock` is only an address hint.
use std::{fs::{self, File}, io::{Read, Seek, SeekFrom, Write}, path::{Path, PathBuf}, time::Duration};
use snapback4_core::{Engine, RuntimeError, Store};
#[cfg(unix)]
use std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt;

pub(crate) struct Owner {
    _sidecar: std::sync::Arc<File>,
    identity: (u64, u64),
    path: PathBuf,
    record: Option<File>,
    reclaimed: Option<String>,
    pub(crate) token_authority: Option<crate::serve::token::Authority>,
}

/// The address `doctor` dials for the server that owns this store: the
/// published loopback address itself, or, for a wildcard bind, that family's
/// loopback on the same port. A published non-loopback address is not followed.
pub(crate) fn inspector_address(store: &Path) -> Option<std::net::SocketAddr> {
    let path = store.canonicalize().ok()?;
    dialable(&address(&path)?)
}

fn dialable(published: &str) -> Option<std::net::SocketAddr> {
    use std::net::{IpAddr, Ipv4Addr, Ipv6Addr, SocketAddr};
    let address = published.strip_prefix("http://")?.parse::<SocketAddr>().ok()?;
    let ip = match address.ip() {
        ip if ip.is_loopback() => ip,
        IpAddr::V4(ip) if ip.is_unspecified() => IpAddr::V4(Ipv4Addr::LOCALHOST),
        IpAddr::V6(ip) if ip.is_unspecified() => IpAddr::V6(Ipv6Addr::LOCALHOST),
        _ => return None,
    };
    (address.port() != 0).then(|| SocketAddr::new(ip, address.port()))
}

fn resolve(store: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    match store.canonicalize() {
        Ok(path) => Ok(path),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
            let parent = store.parent().filter(|p| !p.as_os_str().is_empty()).unwrap_or(Path::new("."));
            snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(parent).map_err(|e| failure(store, e))?;
            Ok(parent.canonicalize().map_err(|e| failure(store, e))?.join(store.file_name().ok_or("store path has no filename")?))
        }
        Err(e) => Err(failure(store, e)),
    }
}

fn failure(path: &Path, reason: impl std::fmt::Display) -> String {
    format!("E_STORE at {}: {reason}; restore a regular store/address file and retry", path.display())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn same_file(a: &fs::Metadata, b: &fs::Metadata) -> bool { a.dev() == b.dev() && a.ino() == b.ino() }

#[cfg(unix)]
fn regular(path: &Path, create: bool, database: &Path) -> Result<File, String> {
    snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(database, path, create, 128)
        .map_err(|e| failure(path, e))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — sidecar flocks fence backend writers and share device ownership without locking SQLite.
pub(crate) fn command(store: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> { acquire(store, false, Duration::ZERO) }
pub(crate) fn device(store: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> { acquire(store, true, Duration::ZERO) }
// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.3 — a restarting host waits for watchdogs to finish group cleanup.
pub(crate) fn host(store: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> { acquire(store, false, Duration::from_secs(5)) }

#[cfg(unix)]
fn acquire(store: &Path, shared: bool, wait: Duration) -> Result<Owner, String> {
    let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + wait;
    for _ in 0..16 {
        let path = resolve(store)?;
        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(path.parent().ok_or("store path has no parent")?)
            .map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?;
        // Stat before opening either auxiliary file; a refused probe creates
        // no database. Only the sidecar lock grants permission to create one.
        let before = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&path).map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?;
        let sidecar = snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_file(&path)
            .map_err(|e| failure(&snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_path(&path), e))?;
        let mut cleanup = None;
        loop {
            let locked = if shared { sidecar.try_lock_shared() } else { sidecar.try_lock() };
            match locked {
                Ok(()) => break,
                Err(fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock) if std::time::Instant::now() < deadline && *cleanup.get_or_insert_with(|| stopped_host(&path)) => std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(25)),
                Err(fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock) => return Err(busy(&path)),
                Err(fs::TryLockError::Error(error)) => return Err(failure(&path, error)),
            }
        }
        if !snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_is_named(&path, &sidecar) { continue; }
        snapback4_core::private_fs::database_file(&path).map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?;
        let database = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&path).map_err(|e| failure(&path, e))?
            .ok_or_else(|| failure(&path, "database disappeared during ownership acquisition"))?;
        if before.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| !same_file(old, &database)) { continue; }
        let mut reclaimed = None;
        let record = if shared { None } else {
            let record_path = path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
            if let Some(pid)=owner_pid(&path) {
                if unsafe {libc::kill(pid as libc::pid_t,0)} == -1 && std::io::Error::last_os_error().raw_os_error()==Some(libc::ESRCH) {
                    reclaimed = Some(format!("reclaimed stale serve.lock: dead PID {pid}; directory {}", path.parent().unwrap().display()));
                }
            }
            let file = regular(&record_path, true, &path)?;
            file.set_len(0).map_err(|e| failure(&record_path, e))?;
            Some(file)
        };
        return Ok(Owner { _sidecar: std::sync::Arc::new(sidecar), identity: (database.dev(), database.ino()), path, record, reclaimed, token_authority: None });
    }
    Err(failure(store, "database path kept changing during ownership acquisition"))
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn acquire(store: &Path, _: bool, _: Duration) -> Result<Owner, String> { Err(failure(store, "database ownership requires Unix")) }

fn stopped_host(path: &Path) -> bool {
    // A CLI owner or a host not yet announced still refuses immediately. A
    // reachable listener does too; only a prior host's cleanup gets a wait.
    // This is a refusal/wait hint, never permission to open the store: the
    // sidecar must still be acquired. Never dial a non-loopback address hint.
    address(path).is_some_and(|published| dialable(&published).is_none_or(|address|
        std::net::TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, Duration::from_millis(50)).is_err()))
}

pub(crate) fn busy(path: &Path) -> String {
    let owner = address(path).map(|address| match owner_pid(path) { Some(pid) => format!("{address} (pid {pid})"), None => address })
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "another CLI command or a server starting up (address pending)".into());
    format!("E_STORE_BUSY at {}: this store is live; owned by {owner}; directory {}; no work was admitted. Stop its `snapback4 dev` or `snapback4 serve` process (or wait for the owning CLI command), rerun this command once, then restart the server. Exclusion holds while <database>-owner is left in place; deleting or replacing it while a process runs ends exclusion and does not stop that process", path.display(),path.parent().unwrap_or(Path::new(".")).display())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(crate) fn address(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
    // A missing/replaced address record cannot grant ownership. Never follow
    // a symlink (including one to the database), nor block on a FIFO.
    let record = path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
    let file = regular(&record, false, path).ok()?;
    let mut text = String::new();
    file.take(1024).read_to_string(&mut text).ok()?;
    let mut lines = text.lines();
    let port = lines.next().and_then(|s| s.parse::<u16>().ok());
    lines.next().map(str::to_owned).or_else(|| port.map(|p| format!("http://127.0.0.1:{p}")))
}

/// The owning host's PID from its address record (`pid N` on the third line).
#[cfg(unix)]
fn owner_pid(path: &Path) -> Option<u32> {
    let record = path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
    let file = regular(&record, false, path).ok()?;
    let mut text = String::new();
    file.take(1024).read_to_string(&mut text).ok()?;
    text.lines().nth(2)?.strip_prefix("pid ")?.parse().ok()
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn owner_pid(_: &Path) -> Option<u32> { None }

#[cfg(not(unix))]
pub(crate) fn address(_: &Path) -> Option<String> { None }

impl Owner {
    pub(crate) fn report_reclaim(&self, verbose: bool) {
        if verbose && let Some(note) = &self.reclaimed { eprintln!("note: {note}"); }
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — unlink only our address inode; the ownership sidecar remains named.
    pub(crate) fn clear_address(&self) {
        #[cfg(unix)]
        if let Some(file) = &self.record {
            let path = self.path.with_file_name("serve.lock");
            if let (Ok(held), Ok(named)) = (file.metadata(), fs::symlink_metadata(&path))
                && same_file(&held, &named) {
                let _ = fs::remove_file(path);
            }
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn worker_lock(&self) -> std::sync::Arc<File> { self._sidecar.clone() }

    pub(crate) fn path(&self) -> Result<&Path, RuntimeError> {
        #[cfg(unix)]
        {
            let meta = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&self.path)
                .map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(failure(&self.path, e)))?;
            if meta.is_some_and(|m| (m.dev(), m.ino()) == self.identity) { return Ok(&self.path); }
        }
        Err(RuntimeError::Store(failure(&self.path, "owned database moved or was replaced; stop and reopen its current path")))
    }

    pub(crate) fn open_store(&self, timeout: Duration) -> Result<Store, RuntimeError> {
        let mut store = Store::open_with_busy_timeout(self.path()?, timeout)?;
        #[cfg(unix)]
        store.retain_ownership(self._sidecar.clone())?;
        Ok(store)
    }

    pub(crate) fn open_engine(&self, bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound, timeout: Duration, now: i64) -> Result<Engine, RuntimeError> {
        let store = self.open_store(timeout)?;
        if store.meta("release")?.is_some() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("this store belongs to a deployment; use `deploy` to activate source and `serve` to run it".into()));
        }
        Engine::new_at(store, bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now)
    }
}

impl Drop for Owner { fn drop(&mut self) { self.clear_address(); } }

pub(crate) fn announce(owner: &mut Owner, address: std::net::SocketAddr) -> Result<(), String> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — live token work runs through the owner, with proof of local store access.
    owner.token_authority = Some(crate::serve::token::publish(owner.path().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?)
        .map_err(|e| failure(&owner.path, e))?);
    let file = owner.record.as_mut().ok_or("device ownership has no server address record")?;
    file.set_len(0).and_then(|_| file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(0))).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    // First-line port is retained for the dev inspector; the third line names the owning process.
    write!(file, "{}\nhttp://{address}\npid {}\n", address.port(), std::process::id()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
}
